Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in geometric theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 552 and the invariant I(A,B) = 230 mod 1.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 552 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the geometric structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 230. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
